# English SEO Master Matrix

Generated 2026-10-15 by `scripts/english-seo-matrix.js` from the QA tool reports (seo-audit, content-duplicate-check, internal-link-audit, frontmatter-check, schema-check, sitemap-check, rendered-site-audit), `docs/matrix/master-matrix.csv`, article front matter and `docs/seo/keyword-targets.json`. **Mode: ZERO-DATA.** No Search Console or analytics data exists yet; the only measured data is the third-party rank-tracker snapshot of 2026-10-14 (positions only), and priorities rest on on-site evidence, that snapshot and the public research recorded in `docs/seo/keyword-evidence.json` and `docs/seo/keyword-universe.json`. Drop a Search Console export at `docs/seo/gsc-performance.csv` to switch to DATA-DRIVEN mode. Full row set: `english-seo-master-matrix.csv` / `.json` (1003 URLs). This file shows every P0-P2 row in full and the P3 rows compactly.

## Keyword universe coverage (zero-data mode)

160 on-topic keywords recorded in `docs/seo/keyword-universe.json` (third-party demand estimates, generated 2026-10-14): 108 core, 10 competitor-brand, 13 other-city, 29 adjacent. Core keywords served by an existing URL: 108; core gaps: 0. Estimated monthly demand covered (core served / core total): AU 750/750, UK 350/350, US 16160/16160, VN 50000/50000. Full mapping: `docs/seo/keyword-universe.md`; silo-level view with demand per cluster and link-integrity recommendations: `docs/seo/topical-map.md` (scripts/topical-map.js).

## Tracked rankings (third-party rank tracker, 2026-10-14)

Ubersuggest project_position_info, Vietnam, desktop: 25 keywords tracked, 15 in the top 100 (top 3: 1, 4-10: 4, 11-20: 5, 21-100: 5; not ranking: 7, unstable: 3). These are measured positions of the best-ranking URL of this domain, not clicks, impressions or traffic; next tracker update 2026-10-21. Positions 4-20 are the quick-win band: the page already ranks, so title, description, lead and internal links are worth hand work (a URL worked on since the snapshot, optimization-log batch with rank_work, waits for the next snapshot). A different URL ranking than the declared owner is a cannibalisation signal: link the pair with natural anchor text, never move content.

## Data limitations (recorded, not blockers)

- ZERO-DATA MODE: no English Google Search Console or GA4 export exists yet (docs/SOURCE-MAP.md); the only measured position data is the third-party rank-tracker snapshot of 2026-10-14 (25 tracked keywords, 15 ranking: positions only, no clicks, impressions or traffic); priorities use on-site evidence, that snapshot, the public SERP snapshots, competitor data and third-party demand estimates recorded in docs/seo/keyword-evidence.json and docs/seo/keyword-universe.json (tool and date on every number).
- DATA-DRIVEN MODE starts automatically when docs/seo/gsc-performance.csv (a Search Console performance export) exists; until then no measured clicks, impressions or positions are claimed.
